# APPENDIX I — S3 MEASURED AGAINST GIT. **490 OWNED · 24 NOT-A-FINDING · 2,638 UNSEEN OF 3,152.**

> **Appended 2026-09-23.** **§§0–12 and Appendices A–H are other agents' work and are not edited
> here**, except for the one cell §2 reserves for this source's status. Appendix H moved S3 out with
> the verdict *"NO DENOMINATOR EXISTS"*. **That verdict is half right and this appendix closes the
> other half: a denominator does exist, git produced it, and S3 has now been measured against it.**
> The answer is **15.55%**, and it is the first figure this source has ever carried that could have
> come out differently if the work had gone differently.
>
> **PINNED AT `199c504149af17d1c76fe3b9f9edaeee7b373978`.** Re-checked at `0ff99df5e` (+7 commits):
> **15.55% → 15.51%.** §I.9.

## I.0 THE HEADLINE

| | |
|---|---:|
| **COUNT IN** — `git rev-list v1.5.5^{commit}..199c50414` | **3,152** |
| **OWNED** — a map document names this commit, and the join is shown | **490** · **15.55%** |
| **NOT-A-FINDING** — it created nothing the map should carry, proven three ways | **24** · **0.76%** |
| **UNSEEN** — no map document accounts for it | **2,638** · **83.69%** |
| **SUM** | **3,152 = COUNT IN** |

**Why this is not another partition.** Every Δ = 0 this file has published for S3 — the nine buckets
`307+103+15+6+3+4+10+2+2 = 452`, and the `78` before it — re-bucketed COUNT IN and summed to COUNT
IN because it had to. **Here the denominator is emitted by `git rev-list` and the numerator is
harvested from the map's own text. Neither can move the other.** Write one more map row citing one
more commit and OWNED goes to 491 while COUNT IN does not move. Land one more uncited commit and
UNSEEN goes to 2,639 while OWNED does not move. **That is the test §1 of `1.6.0-denominator.md`
sets, and this is the first S3 figure to pass it.**

## I.1 THE DENOMINATOR — GIT'S, NOT THE EFFORT'S, AND IT VERIFIES AT TWO PRIOR PINS

`v1.5.5` is an **annotated tag**: `git rev-parse v1.5.5` returns the *tag object*
`d3c504329`, and `v1.5.5^{commit}` returns `a71b21e9f`. Both are written down because confusing them
measures nothing.

```sh
git rev-parse v1.5.5              # -> d3c50432995b8f4b4582380b6c4415308ed44cfc   (TAG OBJECT)
git rev-parse 'v1.5.5^{commit}'   # -> a71b21e9fa0e8a35bd0c86f53f8a79eac2974d05   (COMMIT)
git merge-base --is-ancestor 'v1.5.5^{commit}' 199c50414 && echo ANCESTOR       # -> ANCESTOR
git rev-list a71b21e9f..199c504149af17d1c76fe3b9f9edaeee7b373978 | wc -l       # -> 3152
# CONTROLS, both run:
git rev-list a71b21e9f..a71b21e9f | wc -l                                       # ->    0  (empty range)
git rev-list 199c504149af17d1c76fe3b9f9edaeee7b373978 | wc -l                   # -> 4887  (not zero)
```

**`1.6.0-denominator.md` §5.1's `3,138` reproduces exactly at its own pin**, which is the check that
matters more than the headline:

```sh
git rev-list a71b21e9f..c6a852dfc | wc -l            # -> 3138   (the figure §5.1 publishes)
git rev-list c6a852dfc..199c50414 | wc -l            # ->   14   (3138 + 14 = 3152)
```

**The 452-commit window is 14.3% of this.** `4241fb520` opens it on 2026-09-21, **thirty-two days
after the tag**. Appendix E's `452` also reproduces exactly at *its* pin — it was measured honestly,
it was simply measured over one-seventh of the release:

```sh
git log --since='2026-09-21 05:00' --format=%H 890debf69 | wc -l   # -> 452, as App. E publishes
```

## I.2 THE NUMERATOR — FIVE JOINS, EACH SHOWN, AND NONE OF THEM AUTHORED BY GIT

§0 defines COUNT OUT as *"how many of those reached the master list as a row somebody owns."*
**A commit is OWNED here if any map document names it and the join is exhibited.** Five joins were
run, from strongest to most generous. **Every one is a claim the map makes about a commit; none of
them is a re-bucketing of the commit set.**

| | join | how it is shown | commits |
|---|---|---|---:|
| **J1** | a **table row** in a tracked `.md` cites the SHA | the token sits on a line beginning `\|`, and `git cat-file` resolves it to a commit in the release set | **227** |
| **J2** | any **prose** in a tracked `.md` cites the SHA | same resolution, any line | **371** |
| **J3** | the subject is of the form `type(#NN):` and `#NN` is an item of `BUSBAR-1.6.0.md` | 36 commits, **34** naming a decision the spec contains | **36** |
| **J4** | the message names a **real map row ID** in structured form — `[D36]`, `(M-2)`, `` `CG-49` ``, `GS31:` | the ID is a first-cell of a table row somewhere in `docs/design/*.md` (vocabulary: **1,458** real row IDs) | **117** |
| **J5** | a map document **quotes the subject verbatim** (≥ 25 chars, or its first 40) | the normalised subject is a substring of the normalised corpus | **61** |
| | **UNION — OWNED** | | **490** |

**J3 pays for itself twice.** Two of its 36 commits — `8b5ba2acc` and `eb3c54ed9`, both
`gate(#87):` — name **decision `#87`, which does not exist**; the table runs 1–85. They are still
OWNED, because `#87`'s non-existence is *itself* a mapped row (`X-101`, `X-115` in `1.6.0-MAP.md`
and `1.6.0-THE-LIST.md`: *"A live money gate enforces DECISION #87, which does not exist"*). **The
join holds by a different route than the commit intended, and that is worth more than the count.**

```sh
git grep -c -F '#86' 199c50414 -- docs/design/BUSBAR-1.6.0.md   # -> rc=1, EMPTY: a true zero
git grep -c -F '#85' 199c50414 -- docs/design/BUSBAR-1.6.0.md   # -> 6      the control that proves it
```

**J4 was controlled against a vocabulary that cannot be real.** The same 1,458-ID matcher run
against 199 invented IDs (`ZZ1`…`ZZ199`) returns **0 commits**. A matcher that finds 117 real rows
and 0 invented ones is matching rows, not noise. *(21 of the 117 join through a ≥2-letter or
hyphenated ID — `BOOT-043b`, `ENG27`, `CG-49` — where coincidence is not credible; the other 96 join
only through a one-letter ID such as `M3` or `B1`. They are counted **toward OWNED** on purpose:
every generosity here makes UNSEEN a floor.)*

**The corpus was taken from the object store, never from disk.** The checkout is shared with roughly
twenty agents and carries their uncommitted edits. All **239** tracked `.md` blobs were read through
`git show 199c50414:<path>`.

## I.3 NOT-A-FINDING — THREE CLASSES, EVERY MEMBER PROVEN, AND THE FIVE CLASSES REFUSED

**This bucket is the one that can be used to launder a bad number, so it is kept to 24 of 3,152 —
0.76% — and every member is proven by command, not by reading the subject line.**

| class | test | count |
|---|---|---:|
| **N1** · a merge that introduces no content of its own | `git show --cc --format=` is **empty** — nothing in the merge exists in neither parent | **18** |
| **N2** · a revert and its target, netting exactly to zero | `git diff <target>^ <revert> -- <every path the target touched>` is **empty** | **6** (3 pairs) |
| **N3** · a formatting pass | the whole diff vanishes under `-w` | **1** |
| | **raw total** | **25** |
| | less `31ba5a369`, which a map row cites (**OWNED wins over excused**) | **24** |

**N1 — four of the twenty-two merges were refused.** `9ace4bd2f`, `004f98d89`, `89e5542b6` and
`6db1252d1` carry a **non-empty combined diff**: 429, 1,970, 3,471 and **42,983** bytes of
conflict resolution, hand-written into `Cargo.toml`, `qa/construction.toml`,
`.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `xtask/src/full_gate.rs`, `xtask/src/gates/mod.rs`,
`crates/busbar-core/src/proxy/mod.rs` and `crates/busbar-llm/src/engine/tables.rs`. **Content that
exists in no parent is authored content, and a merge is not a licence to skip it.** They stay in.

**N2 — one of the four named revert pairs was refused.** `68f75fc32` reverts `67672bd33` and leaves
`crates/busbar-plane-a2a/tests/conformance.rs` still changed, so the pair does **not** net to zero
and both commits stay in.

**N3 — one commit in 3,152 qualifies.** `ee6b788a7`, *"auth-admin-tokens: cargo fmt on
mutation_hardening.rs"*, one file, 10+/10−, empty under `-w`.

**THE FIVE CLASSES THAT WERE REFUSED, NAMED SO THAT NOBODY HAS TO GUESS WHAT WAS NOT EXCUSED:**

1. **Commits that wrote the map.** `git rev-list a71b21e9f..199c50414 -- docs/design/` returns
   **579** commits *(control: the same command on a path that does not exist returns 0)*. Excusing
   them would reproduce the exact circularity H.5 faults — the effort deciding that the effort's own
   bookkeeping needs no owner. **384 doc-only commits sit in UNSEEN.**
2. **Pure renames.** Four commits change 0 lines and only move files — including `a39ec8633`,
   *"busbar-plane-admin is not a plane, rename to busbar-core-admin"*, 13 files. **A rename that
   re-classifies a crate is precisely the kind of thing a map exists to carry.**
3. **Reverts that carry a finding.** `0f2395bbf` (*"it is a deadlock, and the watchdog was right"*)
   and `dd6d0828a` (*"tripped plane-purity --check on dev"*) are reverts whose bodies **are** the
   finding. Five of the nine revert-subject commits stay in for this reason.
4. **Formatting reverts.** `d57f71be2`, `8f3c8c5e5`, `b33b429ab` (*"revert incidental formatting of
   files this change does not own"*) change **167 / 561 / 127 non-whitespace lines** under `-w`.
   They are not formatting passes and were not treated as any.
5. **Empty commits.** There are none: **0** of 3,152 touch zero files. The class was tested and
   found empty rather than assumed.

## I.4 COUNT IN = COUNT OUT

```
COUNT IN  ..............................................  3,152
  OWNED            J1∪J2∪J3∪J4∪J5 ......................    490    15.55%
  NOT-A-FINDING    N1∪N2∪N3, less those OWNED ..........     24     0.76%
  UNSEEN           the remainder ......................   2,638    83.69%
                                                          ------
  SUM ..................................................  3,152    == COUNT IN
```

**And the same arithmetic at each strength of join, because one number cannot carry this alone:**

| the join | OWNED | | NOT-A-FINDING | UNSEEN | | sums |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|
| a map **ROW** cites the SHA — the strictest reading of §0's *"a row somebody owns"* | **227** | 7.20% | 25 | **2,900** | 92.01% | ✔ |
| a map row cited it in **any revision** of any markdown | 403 | 12.79% | 25 | 2,724 | 86.42% | ✔ |
| any map **prose** cites the SHA | 371 | 11.77% | 25 | 2,756 | 87.44% | ✔ |
| **+ the four non-SHA joins — PUBLISHED** | **490** | **15.55%** | **24** | **2,638** | **83.69%** | ✔ |
| + every markdown revision ever, deleted documents included | 674 | 21.38% | 23 | 2,455 | 77.89% | ✔ |

**The floor is 7.20% and the absolute ceiling — counting documents that no longer exist — is
21.38%.** `1.6.0-denominator.md` §5.1's **10.39%** sits inside that band and is confirmed: its
measure, re-run at this pin over the same narrow corpus, is **338 of 3,152 = 10.72%**.

## I.5 THE STRUCTURAL FINDING — S3 MEASURED THE ONE-SEVENTH OF THE RELEASE IT WAS STANDING IN

| | commits | OWNED | NOT-A-FINDING | UNSEEN |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| **inside** Appendix E's 452-commit window | 452 | **240 · 53.10%** | 6 | **206 · 45.58%** |
| **outside** it — the other thirty-one days | **2,700** | **250 · 9.26%** | 18 | **2,432 · 90.07%** |

**Two things follow, and the second is the one that matters.**

**First: 206 of the 452 commits Appendix E reports as *"all placed"* are named by no map document at
this pin.** §E.0 is not wrong about what it did — the bodies were read and bucketed — but the
bucketing lived in `docs/design/1.6.0-commit-residue.md`, which was **folded and `git rm`'d at
`2deac0578`**. §E.0 records that deletion itself. **A placement whose only record has been deleted
is not a placement any auditor can see**, and the number puts a size on it.

**Second: outside the window, 2,432 commits — 77% of the entire release — are UNSEEN, and they are
where the code is.** 1,953 of the UNSEEN touch `crates/`, `xtask/`, `scripts/` or `qa/`. **64.10% of
every line this release changed was changed by a commit no map document names:**

| the largest UNSEEN commits | files | lines |
|---|---:|---:|
| `4af1d2f31` *"busbar 1.6.0: composition-root bin"* | 234 | **245,014** |
| `ee4486486` *"engine body — plane extraction + full-codebase audit fixes"* | 366 | **228,269** |
| `ee77896b6` *"structure lint: every inline test body moves to its own file"* | 117 | 48,774 |
| `521d922f4` *"neutral substrate — ArrivalHost vtable, protocol registry, plane-host seam"* | 100 | 31,705 |

**The plane extraction that is the whole of Law 0 is in this table.** S3's ❌ was never about a
missing yardstick. It was about a source that read thirty-six hours of commit bodies and published
it as the coverage of a thirty-three-day release.

## I.6 THE 198 CITATIONS THIS TREE NO LONGER MAKES

**569** release commits are cited by SHA in **some** revision of **some** markdown. **371** are cited
at this pin. **The difference is 198 commits the map once named and no longer does** — `2deac0578`
is the large contributor and it is named in §E.0 as this file's own §12.3 circle closing. It is
recorded here as a number rather than an anecdote: **a citation that was harvested, folded and
deleted leaves the commit UNSEEN, and 198 is how many times that happened.**

## I.7 DOES S3 RECONCILE?

**No — and for the first time the ❌ is a measurement rather than the absence of one.**

- **Appendix H.5's verdict is upheld in its arithmetic and superseded in its diagnosis.** *"No
  denominator exists"* was true of every denominator **the effort wrote**. `git rev-list` is not one
  of those. The denominator existed the whole time.
- **`452 / 78 / Δ 0` remains withdrawn.** Both figures are outputs of the read they claim to check.
- **The replacement is `3,152 / 490 / 2,638 UNSEEN`, and it is falsifiable in both directions.**
- **15.55%** is the honest percentage. **7.20%** if §0's *"a row somebody owns"* is read strictly.

**What would move it, stated so the next reading is comparable rather than new:** rowing the four
commits in §I.5's table would move OWNED by 4 and the changed-line share by **28 points**. There is
no arrangement of the existing documents that moves it, because the shortfall is not bookkeeping.

## I.8 CONTROLLING EVERY ZERO — INCLUDING THE TWO THAT WERE WRONG

**Two instrument failures were caught inside this work and both are recorded, because an appendix
that reports only the greps that worked is not reporting its method.**

1. **A false zero from the shell, exactly as the brief warned.** The revert-pair test was first
   written as `git diff --name-only $X^ $Y -- $files | grep -c .` inside a `for` loop. `$X^` did not
   survive the shell: every call printed `fatal: bad revision '^'` and **every pair reported a
   residual of 0 — a perfect, entirely fabricated result.** Re-run through `git rev-parse
   "$X^{commit}"` in Python with `assert rc == 0` on every call, one of the four pairs does **not**
   net to zero. **The false zero would have excused two more commits than the truth allows.**
2. **A false positive from `awk`, which is the same disease.** Counting whitespace-only commits with
   `awk 'NF==3'` over `--numstat` returns **6**. It is **5**. A git rename pastes the two paths into
   one field — `crates/{busbar-core => busbar-substrate}/src/tests/profile_tests.rs` — so the line
   has five whitespace fields, `NF==3` skips it, the commit's 1+/1− is never added and `e393b9e6d`
   is silently declared unchanged. **`--numstat` is tab-separated and must be split on tabs.** The
   published 5 is the tab-split count.

Every other count was taken against a control known to be non-empty, and each control was run:

| zero or near-zero claimed | the control run beside it | result |
|---|---|---|
| `git rev-list a71b21e9f..a71b21e9f` → 0 | `git rev-list 199c50414` | **4,887** |
| `#86` absent from the spec (`grep -c` → rc 1, empty) | `#85` in the same file | **6** |
| 199 invented row IDs match 0 commits | the 1,458 real row IDs | **117** |
| 0 commits touch zero files | commits touching `crates/` | **1,675** |
| SHAs cited but outside the release set | — | **176**, not zero |
| 18 merges with an empty combined diff | the other four | **429 · 1,970 · 3,471 · 42,983 bytes** |

**`grep -c` exits 1 on no matches, which is indistinguishable from a command that never ran.** Every
`grep -c` above was either paired with a non-empty control on the same file or replaced by Python
with `assert rc == 0` on the subprocess. The harvest, the resolution and all five joins were run in
Python for this reason; `-` and `"` in paths, `\b` semantics that vary by `grep` implementation, and
`"$var:path"` history-modifier expansion were all taken out of the path rather than worked around.

## I.12 THIS APPENDIX CITED SIXTEEN COMMITS AND THEREBY "OWNED" THEM — THE CIRCULARITY, CAUGHT IN THE ACT ON ITSELF

**The instrument harvests SHAs from every tracked `.md` blob. This appendix is a tracked `.md`
blob.** The moment it landed, sixteen commits it names **as examples of the problem** — the four
merges §I.3 refuses to excuse, the revert pairs, the formatting pass, the largest UNSEEN commits —
crossed from UNSEEN into OWNED **because this appendix named them**:

```
004f98d89 0f2395bbf 31ba5a369 521d922f4 67672bd33 68f75fc32 6db1252d1 89e5542b6
8b5ba2acc 8f3c8c5e5 9ace4bd2f b33b429ab d57f71be2 dd6d0828a ee6b788a7 ee77896b6
```

**Naming a commit as an example of being unowned does not own it.** This is H.5's finding — *the
effort writing its own denominator* — reproducing inside the document that re-states H.5's finding,
and it is reported rather than quietly corrected, because §12.3's circle is exactly what this file
keeps closing.

**The published headline is clean by construction.** `490 / 3,152 / 15.55%` was taken at
`199c50414`, a pin at which **Appendix I did not exist in the tree**, and the drift re-check in §I.9
was taken at `0ff99df5e`, also before it landed. **Neither figure contains a single self-citation.**
The contamination can only appear in *re-readings taken after this appendix lands*, and here is its
size, measured at the first such reading:

| at `4fcc39162` | COUNT IN | OWNED | NOT-A-FINDING | UNSEEN |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| counting this appendix's own citations | 3,166 | 513 · **16.20%** | 23 | 2,630 · 83.07% |
| **excluding them — the honest reading** | 3,166 | **500 · 15.79%** | 24 | **2,642 · 83.45%** |
| the difference this appendix makes to its own subject | — | **+13 · +0.41 pt** | −1 | **−12** |

**THE RULE FOR EVERY FUTURE READING, so that the next number is comparable rather than new:**
**exclude this appendix's own text from the corpus.** One line does it, and it is the only
difference between the two rows above:

```python
# when reading docs/design/1.6.0-map-proof.md into the citation corpus:
i = text.find("\n# APPENDIX I —");  assert i > 0;  text = text[:i]
```

**The self-excluded figure has now been taken at three pins spanning 14 commits and two other
agents' landings — 15.55% · 15.51% · 15.79% — and the spread is 0.28 points.** A measurement that
moved with the observer would be measuring the observer. This one does not.

## I.11 THIS APPENDIX'S OWN BANNED-WORD FOOTPRINT — DECLARED

Measured with S9's own regex over this appendix alone:

```sh
python3 -c "import re;P=re.compile(r'defer[a-zA-Z]*|1\.6\.x|\blater\b|out of scope for 1\.6\.0',re.I);\
```

*Subject: **S1, S2b, S4** — the three sources Appendix H found reconciling by partitioning a set

**PIN** = `016422be7904d9ede08c99dd78de855613b3da16` for every §1–§9 reproduction.

measurement** — `199c50414` → `bd6a6a2d7` → `3a3e7ab00` — and **every figure below was re-taken at
each stop and reproduces at all three**: the LEDGER's single `oracle-coverage` line (control
`duplication-census` → 4), the 7 `X-` rows, 83 spec marker lines, M4 absent, 11 `qa/*.toml`, 5 plane
crates, 2,318 corpus rows / 916 recorded, the 100-variant verb table and its 66/34 split, and

**at HEAD**, 2,442 → **2,457**, which is §J.3.4's finding and not a defect.

> **CONTROLLING EVERY ZERO — done, and the two that bit are named.** Every set difference below is
> printed beside a **non-empty intersection**; every `grep -c` zero is re-run against a term known
> present *and* a term known absent. **Three false readings were produced and caught:**
> **(1)** `git ls-tree -d --name-only <rev> -- crates` returns **1**, not 49 — it lists the `crates`
> tree itself, not its children. Caught because "1 crate directory" is absurd; the control is
> `git ls-tree -d --name-only <rev>:crates` → **49**. **(2)** A path-existence walk reported **9**
> dead ratchet paths; **8 were my instrument's fault** — a directory named with a trailing slash
> (`crates/busbar-kernel/src/`) does not match a directory set stored without one. After the fix,
> and a 3-positive/1-negative self-test of the matcher, the honest figure is **1**.
> **(3) I broke the tally with my own prose, and the tally's own command caught me.** §2 derives the
> headline by `grep -c '✅'` **over the last cell of each `| S` row**. My first draft of the S2b and
> S4 cells contained the phrases *"AGAINST THE ✅"* and *"MOVED OUT OF ✅ FOR GOOD"*, and the count
> jumped **11 → 13** — scoring two rows green **for saying they were not**. Rewritten to name the
> glyph in words; the tally reads **11** again, and the eleven are S2a, S2c, S2d, S2e, S2f, S5, S6,
> S7, S8, S9, S10 — **none of them mine**. *A status column parsed by matching a character anywhere
> in the cell is fooled by any cell that discusses the character* — the defect class this document
> exists to catch, produced here by the person writing about it.
> **A `grep -c`
> piped into `head` also masks grep's exit status**, so the "absent term" controls below capture
> `$?` from an unpiped `git grep -l`. POSIX ERE has no `\b`; decision-number matching uses
> `(^|[^0-9])#N([^0-9]|$)`, and bracket expressions are written `[[:space:]]`, never `[\s]`.

mechanically self-enumerating, **C** authored):

| source | denominator found | grade | outcome |

| **S1** | **none exists** — the population is authored inside the source | **C** | **CONSISTENCY CHECK.** Moved out of ✅ for good (§J.2) |
| **S2b** | **the 1.5.5 oracle corpus** — 2,318 declared cells, 916 recorded from a binary with a published `sha256` | **A/B** | **RE-MEASURED**, and the contested verdict **settled** (§J.3) |
| **S4** | **the tree, via the gates that read the ratchets** — 215 scored assertions | **B** | **RE-MEASURED**; the published 2,442 is a **CONSISTENCY CHECK** (§J.4) |

## J.2 S1 — **NO EXTERNAL DENOMINATOR EXISTS. DECLARED A CONSISTENCY CHECK.** ❌

### J.2.1 The finding, stated plainly

**There is no artefact, inside this repository or outside it, that enumerates "the owner questions
1.6.0 has."** The 76 (83 at HEAD) marker lines are annotations the effort wrote into
`BUSBAR-1.6.0.md`, and the master-list components they are measured against — M1, M2, M3 — are
**three sections of that same file**. Membership is a choice made by the effort being measured, so
Δ = 0 is guaranteed before evidence. **That is Grade C, and it cannot be repaired by choosing a
different section.** `51+4+4+5+5+7 = 76` is an identity.

**What S1 does prove, and it is worth keeping:** the file's marker lines are partitioned without
loss or double-count, and the partition re-derives exactly at two pins, 32 days apart in content:

```sh
RE='PARKED|OWNER RULING|needs a ruling|OWNER-LOCKED|REFUSE'
git show ${P}:docs/design/BUSBAR-1.6.0.md | grep -cE "$RE"          # -> 76   lines  (PIN)
git show ${N}:docs/design/BUSBAR-1.6.0.md | grep -cE "$RE"          # -> 83   lines  (HEAD)
git show ${N}:docs/design/BUSBAR-1.6.0.md | grep -oE "$RE" | wc -l  # -> 88   OCCURRENCES
# grep -c counts LINES. PIN is 76 lines / 81 occurrences; HEAD 83 / 88.
# CONTROLS: 'BUSBAR' -> 6 (non-empty); 'zzzz-xyzzy-not-real' -> 0 with grep rc=1 (a real zero).
# per-term at HEAD: OWNER-LOCKED 57 · REFUSE 13 · PARKED 11 · OWNER RULING 6 · needs a ruling 1
```

**That is a consistency check. It is not a completeness proof, and §2 no longer publishes it as
one.**

### J.2.2 The one external measurement S1 admits — and it is a lower bound, not a verdict

54 of the 83 marker lines are **numbered decision rows** (`| N | …`); 29 are prose, headings or
table cells. A decision number is a stable identifier, and the enforcement machinery cites decision
numbers in its own source — `reachability` names *"the #48 roster"*, `no-float-money` names
*"#77.8"*, `seal-witness` names *"#65/#73"*, `plane-pricing-blindness` names *"#43/#71"*. **So one
externally checkable question exists: does anything that executes cite this decision?**
`git grep` reads the tree and the spec cannot conceal the answer — Grade B.

| scope searched | decisions cited | cited by nothing |
|---|---|---|
| `crates/` + `xtask/` (production code + gates) | **33** of 54 | **21** |
| `crates/ xtask/ scripts/ .github/ qa/` (everything that executes) | **38** of 54 | **16** |

```sh
for d in $(cat decisions.txt); do
  out=$(git grep -lE "(^|[^0-9])#${d}([^0-9]|\$)" ${N} -- crates xtask scripts .github qa); rc=$?
  printf '#%s %s rc=%s\n' "$d" "$(printf '%s' "$out" | grep -c .)" "$rc"
done
# CONTROLS, unpiped so grep's exit status survives:
#   #48  -> 16 files, rc=0      (known cited — `reachability` names the #48 roster)
#   #999 ->  0 files, rc=1      (cannot exist — a REAL zero, not a masked one)
```

**⛔ AND THIS IS WHY IT IS A LOWER BOUND, NOT A SCORE. I spot-checked three of the sixteen and all
three are enforced under a different spelling.** Reporting "16 unenforced decisions" would have been
a manufactured red:

| decision | cited? | but its subject is… |
|---|---|---|
| **#49** *"the kernel knows NO plane or transport STRINGS"* | no | enforced by `construction:lean-core`, which is **FAIL today** — *"17 string literal(s) in `['busbar-kernel','busbar-unit-transport-key']` naming a dialect … (ceiling 0)"* |
| **#62** *"a mid-stream cut is NOT a refund"* | no | `refund` carries tests in `busbar-contract/src/tests/records_tests.rs` and `busbar-kernel-audit/src/tests/amend_tests.rs` |
| **#82** *"the audit chain is SIGNED IN CORE; admin EXPOSES it for pull"* | no | `GetAuditHead` / `GetAuditRange` / `GetAuditKeys` are live variants in `crates/busbar-core-admin/src/verb.rs`, wired in `crates/busbar/src/root/units_admin/mod.rs` |

**38 of 54 is a citation footprint. It is real, external and could have come out differently — but
it measures whether a number was written down, not whether a rule is enforced.** It is recorded as
the only external handle S1 has, and it is **not** offered as S1's COUNT OUT.

### J.2.3 The reference scheme cannot uniquely name a row — so even the consistency check is weak

Appendix H.1.2 found M2 and M3 colliding on **three** numbers. Re-measured at HEAD the defect is
structural and larger: `BUSBAR-1.6.0.md` carries **86** distinct small-integer ids across its
numbered tables and `### N.` headings, and **18 of them resolve to more than one place.**

| id | table rows at | `### N.` heading at | distinct places |
|---|---|---|---|
| **1** | L19, L319, L1825, L3136 | L1198 | **5** |
| **2** | L20, L320, L1826, L3137 | L1207 | **5** |
| **3** | L21, L321, L1827, L3138 | L1222 | **5** |
| **11** | L329, L3146 | L1286 | **3** |
| **12** | L330, L3147 | L1375 | **3** |
| **13** | L331, L3148 | L1477 | **3** |
| | | **18 ambiguous of 86** | **worst = 5** |

**Bucket F's citations use exactly this scheme** — *"M2 item 1"*, *"M3 §12"*, *"M4 §3"*. A citation
of the form *"item 12"* names **three** rows in one file. **A denominator that cannot uniquely
address its own members is not a denominator even for a consistency check.**

### J.2.4 Bucket F re-verified at my pin — still 4 of 7

Every fact Appendix H.2 recorded holds at `199c50414` and at both subsequent stops:

- `docs/design/1.6.0-owner-decisions.md` (**M4**) — **ABSENT** (control: `BUSBAR-1.6.0.md` PRESENT).
  Three of the seven citations are to M4 and one cites M4 **and nothing else**.
- The `sha2 -> cpufeatures` pair is **still both**: `#### PARKED FOR THE OWNER — …` at **L2879**
  and `#### OWNER RULING 2026-09-22 — … IS NOT AN I/O REACH (settles the row below)` at **L2767**.
  The ruling that settles it sits **112 lines above** the park it settles, and the park is unstruck.
- *"M2 item 5-adjacent"* is not a row id; *"M2 items 9/14"* is §1.1(2)'s one finding counted twice.

**VERDICT — S1 is a CONSISTENCY CHECK and is moved out of ✅ permanently.** It proves its own file
partitions without loss. It proves nothing about omission, because the list and the check were
written by the same hand, and its citation scheme is ambiguous at 18 of 86 ids. **No denominator
for S1 exists that is not part of the source, and none is going to be found.**

## J.3 S2b — **THE CONTESTED VERDICT IS SETTLED, AND A REAL DENOMINATOR EXISTS.** ❌ as published

### J.3.1 THE CONTEST, SETTLED BY ONE COMMAND — `1.6.0-MAP.md` Sweep 1 IS RIGHT

Two live documents disagreed. Appendix C.1 published ✅; `1.6.0-MAP.md` Sweep 1 scored the same
source **❌ UNRECONCILED** because *"zero LEDGER rows cite it"*. **That factual claim is true, it is
still true at three stops today, and it settles the dispute against Appendix C.1.**

```sh
git show ${N}:docs/design/1.6.0-LEDGER.md | grep -nE 'oracle-coverage'
# -> 921:`1.6.0-commit-residue` · `1.6.0-ratchet-census` · `1.6.0-oracle-coverage` · `1.6.0-map-proof` ·
#    ONE line, and it is not a table row:
git show ${N}:docs/design/1.6.0-LEDGER.md | sed -n '921p' | grep -qE '^\|' && echo ROW || echo "NOT A ROW"
# -> NOT A ROW.  Its heading two lines up is "**LANDED, NOT YET FOLDED:**" —
#    the LEDGER's own housekeeping saying this source has NOT been folded.
```

**The controls, and they are what make the 1 believable:**

| probe | lines in `1.6.0-LEDGER.md` | |
|---|---|---|
| `duplication-census` | **4** | a source known folded — the control Sweep 1 itself names |
| `done-readout` | **13** | folded, `DR0`–`DR16` |
| `gate-sweep` | **5** | folded, `GS0`–`GS35` |
| `instrument-audit` | **2** | folded, `IA1`–`IA13` |
| **`oracle-coverage`** | **1** | **and that one says "NOT YET FOLDED"** |
| `zzzz-not-a-real-token-xyzzy` | **0**, grep rc=1 | a real zero, for calibration |

**Do not average them and do not pick the friendlier one: Sweep 1's ❌ is correct on its own claim,
and Appendix C.1's `4 + 7 + 14 = 25` was a partition identity that never left the source.** The
published `OUT = 25` is **withdrawn**.

**The chronology matters and it is not circular.** `X-1`…`X-7` — the rows Appendix H.4 counted as
S2b's external landing — were raised **by Sweep 1**, i.e. **by the ❌ verdict**, on 2026-09-22/23,
**after** Appendix C.1 published ✅. So the ✅ H.4 re-measured was earned by rows that the ❌ created.
**Both documents were right about different objects**: zero rows in the **LEDGER** (Sweep 1), seven
rows in the **MAP** (H.4). What is not defensible is Appendix C.1's original 25, which cited
neither.

**And the MAP is Grade C.** `1.6.0-MAP.md` was born `aa2b0ca97`, 2026-09-22 20:13 — inside the
window, authored by the effort. Counting `X-1`…`X-7` as S2b's denominator swaps one authored list
for another. **A real denominator has to come from outside the paperwork entirely.**

### J.3.2 THE REAL DENOMINATOR — the 1.5.5 oracle corpus, and why it fits

`1.6.0-oracle-coverage.md` is a document **about oracle coverage**. Its subject therefore has an
external referent that no section count can substitute for: **the corpus itself, and the recording
of the 1.5.5 binary.** `1.6.0-denominator.md` established both and they reproduce here exactly:

```sh
git show ${N}:testing/shadow-oracle/golden/1.5.5/ledger.tsv | wc -l                    # -> 2318
git show ${N}:…/ledger.tsv | awk -F'\t' '{print $2}' | sort | uniq -c                   # -> 916 PASS  1402 SKIP
git show ${N}:testing/shadow-oracle/golden/1.5.5/meta.json
# -> "recorded": 916, "binary_sha256": "84bde0a047fe368b661e9f6f14b2916390c40b71a6d91cdff1dbfa350aaf80e7"
```

**Why it fits.** The 916 values were transcribed from a binary the effort did not build and cannot
change, pinned by a published digest — **Grade A for the values recorded**. The corpus enumeration
is produced by reading the tree, and the effort cannot conceal a cell from it — **Grade B for
membership**. Neither is a list somebody wrote to be reconciled against.

**The second half of the denominator is the closed verb table**, and it is stronger than it looks:
`crates/busbar-core-admin/src/verb.rs` is an exhaustively-matched enum whose 66 legacy rows are
*"mechanically derived from 1.5.5's `openapi.json` at the tag"* and held there by
`tests/table_matches_openapi.rs`, which *"fails the build if this list and the committed fixture
ever disagree, by even one operation or one scope"*. **It partitions exactly:**

```
LEGACY_VERBS 66 + NEW_VERBS 18 + LEDGER_VERBS 5 + AUDIT_VERBS 3 + NAMED_SURFACES 8 = 100
KernelVerb enum variants = 100, distinct = 100
```

### J.3.3 THE RE-MEASUREMENT — 66 of 66 preserved, 34 of 100 uncovered

| group | size | has a recorded 1.5.5 cell | **no cell** |
|---|---|---|---|
| `LEGACY_VERBS` (derived from the tag) | 66 | **66** | **0** |
| `NEW_VERBS` | 18 | 0 | **18** |
| `LEDGER_VERBS` | 5 | 0 | **5** |
| `AUDIT_VERBS` | 3 | 0 | **3** |
| `NAMED_SURFACES` (non-admin) | 8 | 0 | **8** |
| | **100** | **66** | **34** |

```sh
# distinct op names in RECORDED admin.ops cells, against the closed enum
# -> cells: 67   verbs: 100   INTERSECTION (the control): 66   uncovered: 34
# -> cell ops that are not a verb (the other-side control): ['admin-token']  — a setup cell, exactly 1
```

**Two findings, and they run in opposite directions.**

**(1) The preservation half is perfect and should be said out loud: 66 of 66.** Every operation
1.5.5 published has a cell recorded from the 1.5.5 binary. **This is the one number in S2b that is
Grade A on both sides, and it is green.**

**(2) `X-1`'s published `26` re-derives exactly — under the right scope.** X-1 reads *"**26 verb.rs
ops** with no oracle cell"*. `18 + 5 + 3 = 26` is precisely the **admin-scoped** uncovered set; the
remaining 8 are `NAMED_SURFACES`, which `verb.rs`'s own doc excludes from the admin table (*"not
part of the admin `openapi.json` table … they are not admin-scoped mutations at all"*). **X-1 is
CONFIRMED at 26 admin-scoped, and the full figure including non-admin surfaces is 34.**

**(3) `X-3`'s published `3 of 5` re-derives under neither reading, and the roster is Grade B:**

```sh
git ls-tree -d --name-only ${N}:crates | grep -cE '^busbar-plane-'    # -> 5   (control: 49 crate dirs)
```

| plane crate | cells **declared** | cells **recorded** |
|---|---|---|
| `busbar-plane-llm` | 142 | **133** |
| `busbar-plane-a2a` | 468 | **0** |
| `busbar-plane-mcp` | 912 | **0** |
| `busbar-plane-decision` | **0** | **0** |
| `busbar-plane-streaming` | **0** | **0** |

**Zero DECLARED cells: 2 of 5. Zero RECORDED cells: 4 of 5. `X-3` says 3 of 5 and neither column
says 3.** The row that owns §1.6 carries a number that is true of nothing.

```sh
# CONTROL, because "no streaming cells" is exactly the shape of a false zero:
grep -ciE 'stream|voice|decision|jev' ledger.tsv     # -> 730  — the grep is NOT blind
# …but all 730 are streaming SHAPES inside other families, and 657 of them are SKIP:
#   mcp 542 · a2a 108 · llm 73 · route.failover 3 · neutrality 1 · http.crosscut 1 · hooks 1 · billing 1
# No cell id has `streaming|` or `decision|` as its family. The zero is real.
```

**`X-5` was already found wrong by §C.1.5 (30 of 41 published against 33 of 40 measured). So of the
four `X-` rows with a mechanically checkable number, `X-1` holds and `X-3` and `X-5` do not.**

> **`X-6` and `X-7` ARE MONEY AND ARE PARKED, NOT SCORED.** *"two money reads carrying
> `resolves: false`"* and *"14 named money behaviours with no cell at all"* were **not** re-derived
> here. Money is binary and a money figure without an external denominator is a **PARK**, so they
> are parked to the owner rather than counted in either direction. **Two of the seven rows that own
> S2b's open items are unverified money claims.**

**VERDICT — S2b does not reconcile as published.** `4 + 7 + 14 = 25` is an identity and is
withdrawn; the contest is settled **against** the ✅; and measured against a denominator the effort
did not write, the honest statement is **66 of 66 preserved, 34 of 100 uncovered, 2 of 5 planes
with no corpus at all** — real numbers that could each have come out differently. **The row is ❌,
and the ❌ is more informative than the ✅ it replaces.**

## J.4 S4 — **A RATCHET IS TRUE ABOUT THE TREE. MEASURED THERE, IT IS 215 CLAIMS, NOT 2,442.** ⚠

### J.4.1 The corrected count-in, independently re-derived for the fourth time

| part | what it counts | §6.3 | **re-derived here** |
|---|---|---|---|
| **A** | array-of-table rows, all `qa/*.toml` | 1,315 | **1,315** ✔ (**14** arrays) |
| **B** | entries in `construction.toml` exception arrays | 131 (27 arrays) | **129** (**26** arrays) ✘ |
| **C** | permitted sites bought by non-zero ratchets | 30 | **30** ✔ (17+4+5+2+1+1) |
| **D** | `plane-purity-strict.toml` held-open lines | 968 | **968** ✔ (61+13+377+456+33+0 **+28**) |
| | **S4 COUNT IN at `PIN`** | **2,444** | **2,442** |

**Part B is 129.** Exactly one member of §6.3's own key families is a **dict of arrays** —
`rules.plane-no-money.allowlist."crates/busbar-llm/src/unit/verify.rs"` — and counting the dict as
an array *and* its contents as an array yields 27/131. **The 2-row gap is that double count, and
Δ = 0 survived on two rows that are in no file.** This is now the **fourth** independent
derivation (`1.6.0-ratchet-census.md` §R.1, `1.6.0-map-audit.md` D1, App. H.6.1, and this walk).

**Part D is 968 to the line:** `categories` 0+61+13+377+456+33 = 940, `test-reach` 24+1+3+0 = 28.

**The +9 confirmed, to the record.** `rules.no-test-doubles-in-production.known_sites` is a TOML
**table of 14 records**, invisible to an array walk — and the split is exact at both pins:

```
known_sites records = 14   verdicts = {'double': 5, 'real': 9}   max_doubles = 5
```

The 5 `double` are already bought by `max_doubles = 5` in Part C; **the 9 `real` are counted by no
part.** So: **2,444 published · 2,442 on the record · 2,451 fully honest.**

### J.4.2 AND THE COUNT-IN IS NOT STABLE — it grew 15 rows while this was being written

**A denominator that moves with the work is not a denominator.** A new ratchet file landed after
`PIN`, and the arrays inside the existing ones grew:

```sh
git ls-tree -r --name-only ${P} -- qa | grep -c '\.toml$'    # -> 10
git ls-tree -r --name-only ${N} -- qa | grep -c '\.toml$'    # -> 11   (+ qa/unconstructed.toml)
```

| | `PIN` | HEAD |
|---|---|---|
| A | 1,315 (14 arrays) | **1,323** (15 arrays) |
| B | 129 (26 arrays) | **136** (26 arrays) |
| C · D | 30 · 968 | 30 · 968 |
| **count-in** | **2,442** | **2,457** |

**The population S4 partitions is authored by the effort and grows as the effort works.** Any
Δ = 0 over it is an identity taken over a moving set.

### J.4.3 THE QUESTION THE BRIEF ASKS — what external thing is a ratchet true about?

**A ratchet asserts something falsifiable about the tree: "rule R is violated at no more than N
sites."** That claim is checkable by an instrument that reads the tree rather than the ledger, and
such instruments exist and are in this repository — **the gates themselves**. That is Grade B, and
it is the denominator S4 has been missing.

**The decisive fact is that a ratchet FILE ENTRY is not a CLAIM.** The 2,442 entries are **inputs**
to the gates; the gates emit a far smaller number of **scored assertions** about the tree. Run
read-only (no `--write`, no `sync`, no `--approve`), from the prebuilt `target/debug/xtask`:

| gate | its ratchet file | file entries | **scored rows** | PASS | **FAIL** |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `construction` | `construction.toml` | 129 + 30 | **113** | 101 | **12** |
| `instance-noun-neutrality` | `instance-noun-neutrality.toml` | **723** | **28** | 4 | **24** |
| `unconstructed` | `unconstructed.toml` | 19 | **21** | 20 | **1** |
| `reachability` | `reachability.toml` | — | **20** | 16 | **4** |
| `kind-isolation` | `kind-isolation.toml` | **537** | **12** | 7 | **5** |
| `plane-pricing-blindness` | `plane-pricing-blindness.toml` | — | **11** | 6 | **5** |
| `plane-purity-strict` | `plane-purity-strict.toml` | **968** | **10** | 10 | 0 |
| | | | **215** | **164** | **51** |

**`instance-noun-neutrality.toml` holds 723 `[[leak]]` rows and its gate scores 28. `qa/kind-isolation.toml`
holds 537 rows and its gate scores 12.** The ratio is the finding: **2,442 entries produce 215
falsifiable assertions.**

**And the instrument can say NO — 51 times, right now.** That is the control that makes the 164
PASS mean anything:

```
construction:one-pick-site           FAIL  4 call site(s) of `pick_among(` (ceiling 2)
construction:loc-ceilings:kernel:teller FAIL  teller.rs 685 line(s) (ceiling 661)
construction:loc-ceilings:caps-contract FAIL  busbar-contract 6836 (ceiling 5652)
construction:loc-ceilings:union      FAIL  52494 + 6836 + 268 = 59598 (ceiling 58870)
construction:lean-core               FAIL  17 string literal(s) naming a dialect (ceiling 0)
construction:plane-no-money          FAIL  `priced_from_ms` at crates/busbar-llm/src/unit/meter.rs:490
construction:one-pricing-site        FAIL  1 pricing-entry call site outside the reviewed homes
construction:no-test-doubles…doubles FAIL  7 against ratchet 5
kind-isolation:matrix                FAIL  38391 hit(s) over 267 cell(s), 1813 file(s) scanned
…                                          51 FAIL / 164 PASS over 215 scored rows
```

### J.4.4 The numbers moved since Appendix D, which is the proof they are measurements

Appendix D.2 printed `KEY 139 ≤ 377` and **owed 460 / headroom 508**. Re-run today:

```
plane-purity-strict:category:KEY      PASS   141 <= 377          (was 139)
PATH-INCLUDE 0≤0 · SYMBOL 9≤61 · TYPE 0≤13 · DIALECT 312≤456 · BACKWARDS 0≤33
test-reach llm 0≤24 · mcp 0≤1 · a2a 0≤3 · voice 0≤0
=> measured 462 of 968 held-open lines; HEADROOM 506        (App. D said 508)
```

**`loc-ceilings:caps-contract` moved too — App. D.3 measured `busbar-contract` at 6,815; it is
6,836 today.** These figures track the tree, so they answer the brief's question in the affirmative:
**they could have come out differently, and in two places they did.**

**506 of the 968 held-open lines are headroom nobody can trip** — and `construction`'s own
`ceiling-slack` row, whose rule reads *"a ceiling above its measurement is room nobody voted for"*,
is **FAIL reporting exactly 1**, because it does not read `plane-purity-strict.toml`. App. D.3
named this; it is re-confirmed and re-measured here at 506.

**Three rows declare their own vacuity in their own output**, which is to the gate's credit and is
also the shape of the defect:

```
loc-ceilings:kernel:slice   PASS  0 line(s) (ceiling 0) -- no matching file under busbar-kernel/src yet (vacuous 0)
loc-ceilings:unit-verbs     PASS  0 line(s) (ceiling 0) -- busbar-unit-verbs does not exist yet (vacuous 0)
hold-discipline:cancellation-before-await  FAIL  vacuous: no `.await` found inside a route-step function in scope
```

**And the ratchets name one subject that is not in the tree**, found by an independent path walk and
confirmed by the gate: of **891** path-shaped strings across all `qa/*.toml`, **890 resolve** and
**1 does not** — `crates/busbar-export-*` in `[gate.plugin_kinds].export`, a family glob matching
no crate. `construction:ceiling-census` is **FAIL** reporting **3** census problems of exactly this
class, and the file's own comment at L1256 already calls it *"the fourth table that named a kind
that does not exist."* **The rule is inert regardless: `kinds = ["plane", "hook", "auth"]` does not
list `export`.**

### J.4.5 The verdict

**VERDICT — the published `2,444 · 2,444 · Δ 0` is a CONSISTENCY CHECK and is moved out of ✅ for
good.** Appendix D's seven buckets classify **the source's own rows**; none of `(a)`–`(g)` is
*"reached the master list"*; bucket `(d)` is **1,543 rows of live open work with no map row**; and
**§6.4's `COUNT OUT = 2` was never withdrawn and is still the figure §0 asks for.** Stated as §0
defines the terms: **IN 2,442 · OUT 2 · Δ 2,440.**

**What S4 is worth, stated positively:** the partition is arithmetically sound, it is reproducible,
and it is the only complete inventory of the ratchet surface anyone has built. **Measured against
the tree instead of against itself, the honest figures are: 215 scored assertions, 51 RED today,
506 of 968 held-open lines unreachable, 1 of 891 paths dead, 3 rows self-declared vacuous.** Those
are measurements. **2,444 was not.**

*Not a money figure: ratchet and exception rows, no billed byte moves. No PARK is owed for S4
itself — but `construction:plane-no-money` and `construction:one-pricing-site` are **both FAIL on
the money path today** and are named here because a census that walked past them would be the
defect this document is about.*

## J.5 THE TALLY — UNCHANGED AT `11 of 15`, AND NOT TOUCHED BY THIS APPENDIX

**None of S1, S2b or S4 carried a ✅ when this appendix opened, so the headline does not move and
this appendix does not write it.** The tally is single-writer and §2's command remains the only
place it is derived:

```sh
awk '/^## 2\. THE RECONCILIATION TABLE/{f=1} /^\*\*THE HONEST TALLY/{f=0} f' \
  docs/design/1.6.0-map-proof.md | grep -cE '^\| S'                                    # -> 15
awk '/^## 2\. THE RECONCILIATION TABLE/{f=1} /^\*\*THE HONEST TALLY/{f=0} f' \
  docs/design/1.6.0-map-proof.md | awk -F'|' '/^\| S/{print $(NF-1)}' | grep -c '✅'    # -> 11
```

**What this appendix does change is the column that matters more — how a ✅ was earned:**

| # | status | earned how, after this appendix |
|---|---|---|
| **S1** | ❌ | **arithmetically only.** No external denominator exists; **declared a CONSISTENCY CHECK** (§J.2) |
| **S2b** | ❌ | **arithmetically only** as published; contest **settled against the ✅**; re-measured externally at 66/66 and 34/100 (§J.3) |
| **S4** | ⚠ | **arithmetically only**; the 2,442 is a **CONSISTENCY CHECK**; externally it is **215 claims, 51 red** (§J.4) |
| S2a · S2c · S7 · S8 | ✅ | **evidentially** — each against a document or file that is not its source (App. H.3, H.8) |
| S5 · S6 · S10 | ✅ | **evidentially** — `json` parses of registers the effort did not enumerate (App. B) |
| S2d · S2e · S2f | ✅ | **evidentially** — each against `1.6.0-LEDGER.md` rows, which are not the source (App. G) |
| S9 | ⚠ inside a ✅ | **evidentially on 9, not on 21** — H.8 stands; unchanged here |
| S3 | ❌ | **arithmetically only** — not this appendix's surface |

**`11` carry a ✅. Of those eleven, ten are evidentially earned and one (S9) is evidentially earned
on a smaller number than it publishes.** The four without a ✅ — **S1, S2b, S3, S4** — are
arithmetically earned and nothing more, and three of them are now **declared** consistency checks
rather than merely observed to be partitions.

> **THE STRUCTURAL FINDING IS UNREPAIRED AND THIS APPENDIX STRENGTHENS IT.** H.9 said a completeness
> proof needs a denominator the work did not create, and that this release lacks one. **Two of the
> three sources here now have one** — S2b has the 1.5.5 corpus (Grade A) and the closed verb table
> (Grade B); S4 has the tree via its gates (Grade B). **S1 has none and cannot be given one**,
> because its population is an authored list inside the file it is measured against. **Where an
> external denominator was found, the number it produced was worse than the partition it replaced
> — 34 of 100 uncovered, 51 of 215 red — and that is the point. A green number nobody can trust is
> worth less than a red one they can.**

## J.6 THIS APPENDIX'S OWN BANNED-WORD FOOTPRINT — DECLARED, NOT HIDDEN

Measured with S9's own regex, Appendix J returns **3 lines / 3 occurrences**, and all three are bucket
**(a)** under §9.3 (*"a quotation of the ban itself, or the gate that enforces it"*). **Zero are
bucket (c).** The split:

| what | lines | occurrences |
|---|---|---|
| the ban's own regex, quoted once in the reproduce command just below | **1** | **1** |
| the literal path `scripts/no-deferral.waivers` — named in the sentence below to contrast this appendix's footprint with App. H.7's, and once more in this very table row, because the instrument cannot be cited by another spelling | **2** | **2** |
| | **3** | **3** |

**The count includes this section counting itself** — describing the path in the table above cost
one further occurrence, taking 2 to 3. A first draft of §J.6 claimed **0 / 0**, which was false and
was caught by running the measurement instead of asserting it. That is G56's point twice over:
*the banned-word count is not monotone in the thing it measures*, so it cannot be a gate.
Reproduce:

```sh
python3 -c "import re;P=re.compile(r'defer[a-zA-Z]*|1\.6\.x|\blater\b|out of scope for 1\.6\.0',re.I);\
t=open('docs/design/1.6.0-map-proof.md').read().split(chr(10));\
i=next(k for k,l in enumerate(t) if l.startswith('# APPENDIX J'));h=t[i:];\
print(sum(1 for l in h if P.search(l)), sum(len(P.findall(l)) for l in h))"
# -> 3 3
# CONTROL, the same regex over the whole file: 178 lines / 222 occurrences — the regex is NOT inert.
```

**Not one of the three is a real one**, and the footprint is this small only because §J.2–§J.4 name
no instrument whose identifier carries a banned term — unlike App. H.7, which could not state its
finding without naming `scripts/no-deferral.waivers`.

> **ONE INTERACTION THIS APPENDIX OWES APPENDIX I, REPORTED RATHER THAN SILENTLY CAUSED.** Appendix
> I's footprint command slices `t[i:]` from its own heading **to the end of the file**, so with
> Appendix J appended below it that window now also counts these three occurrences and will print
> **4 / 4**, not the `1 / 1` recorded there. **Appendix I is not this appendix's surface and was not
> edited.** The fix is one bounded slice — stop the window at the next `# APPENDIX` heading — and it
> belongs to whoever owns that section. **An open-ended window over a shared file measures whatever
> lands after it**, which is the same defect class §2 records for a tally written in more than one
> place.

---

*Appendix J measured on `consolidated/1.6.0`. `PIN = 016422be7` for every §1–§9 reproduction;
`HEAD = 199c50414` at start, re-checked at `bd6a6a2d7` and `3a3e7ab00` after the tree moved twice
mid-measurement. Every zero printed above was taken against a control that is not zero, and the three
false readings this appendix produced — the `ls-tree` depth error, the trailing-slash path mismatch,
and a `✅` written into a status cell that the tally's own command then counted — are recorded in the
banner rather than quietly fixed. **Nothing was blessed, re-recorded
or regenerated: no golden, no `accepted-differences.json`, no `accepted-gaps.json`, no
`openapi.json`, and no `qa/*.toml` — all eleven ratchet files were parsed with `tomllib` from the
object store and written by nothing.** The seven gates were run read-only from the prebuilt
`target/debug/xtask` with `--format=tsv` and no `--write`, no `sync` and no `--approve`. A number
that does not reproduce is a defect in this appendix and should be reported rather than worked
around.*
